According to legal provisions, for other risks beyond our control, you shall bear full responsibility, including but not limited to:",line124:"(1) Risks of data loss, leakage, etc., caused by force majeure or other unexpected events;",line125:"(2) Risks of damage due to the mismatch of this product with your terminal device model, or due to poor network signals or low bandwidth that you are using;",line126:"(3) Risks caused by others stealing your information or results after dissemination;",line127:"(4) Any risks arising from factors beyond our control.",line128:"5. Upon receiving reports or complaints from others and confirming that you have violated this agreement or engaged in other illegal activities, we have the right to delete the relevant content at any time and impose penalties based on the severity of the situation, including warnings, restricting functions, suspending services, banning accounts, or even closing accounts, and to publicly announce the results of the handling. If your actions violate national laws and regulations, we will continue to retain relevant information and report to the relevant authorities as required by law. You shall bear all legal responsibilities arising therefrom, and in serious cases, you may even face criminal liability.",line129:"6. If you violate relevant laws and regulations or the provisions of this agreement, resulting in losses to us, claims from other users or any third party, or penalties from administrative authorities, you shall fully compensate us, other users, or relevant third parties for the actual losses incurred, including but not limited to reasonable attorney fees, travel expenses, and other legal rights protection costs.",line130:"7. You understand and agree that if we do not enforce or exercise any statutory rights or remedies included in this agreement (or which we are entitled to under any applicable laws), it shall not be deemed a formal automatic waiver of our rights, and these rights or remedies shall still be valid for us. Our waiver of rights stipulated in this agreement in case of your negligence or breach shall not be construed as a waiver of our rights regarding your other or future similar negligence or breach.",line131:"8. Reporting and Complaint Channels",line132:"1. In the following circumstances, anyone has the right to report to us, and we will promptly accept, process, and provide feedback on the handling results:",line133:"(1) Any infringement of intellectual property rights by other entities discovered while using this product;",line134:"(2) Any violations of laws and regulations by other entities discovered while using this product;",line135:"(3) Any actions by other entities that harm the legal rights of others discovered while using this product;",line136:"(4) Any other violations by other entities that need addressing discovered while using this product;",line137:"(5) Any behavior by other entities violating the provisions of this agreement discovered while using this product;",line138:"(6) Any other issues you believe are necessary to reflect to us.",line139:"2. Reporting Channels",line140:"(1) Contact Email: {email}",line141:"(2) WeChat Official Account: Chuan Shen Community",line142:"9. Dispute Resolution and Applicable Legal Provisions",line143:"1. The establishment, execution, interpretation, and resolution of disputes of this agreement shall be subject to Chinese law and governed by Chinese courts.",line144:"2. If the parties have any disputes regarding the content or execution of this agreement, both parties shall strive to resolve them through friendly negotiation; if negotiation fails, either party may file a lawsuit with the people's court at the location of the company."},policy:{line1:"Update Date: October 20, 2023",line2:"Effective Date: October 20, 2023",line3:'Your trust is very important to us. We are well aware of the significance of personal information to you, and we will take appropriate security protection measures as required by laws and regulations to the best of our ability to protect the security and controllability of your personal information. Therefore, Chuan Shen Community (hereafter referred to as "we" or "this platform") has formulated this "Chuan Shen Community Privacy Policy" (hereafter referred to as "this policy" or "this privacy policy") to help you fully understand how we collect, use, share, store, and protect your personal information, as well as how you can manage your personal information during your use of our platform\'s products and services.',line4:"Before you start using our services, please carefully read and understand this policy, especially the terms highlighted in bold/bold underline, to ensure that you fully understand and agree before you begin using. We will strive to explain the professional terms involved in this policy in a concise and accessible manner to facilitate your understanding.",line5:"This privacy policy will help you understand the following contents:",line6:"1. How we collect and use your personal information",line7:"2. How we share, transfer, and publicly disclose your personal information",line8:"3. How we store and protect your personal information",line9:"4. How you can manage your personal information",line10:"5. How we use cookies and similar technologies",line11:"6. Special provisions regarding minor user information",line12:"7. How this privacy policy is updated",line13:"8. How to contact us",line14:"1. How we collect and use your information",line15:"When you use our services, we need/may need to collect and use your personal information, which includes the following two types:",line16:"1. Basic functionality required to provide our services, for which you must authorize us to collect and use the necessary information. If you refuse to provide the corresponding information, you will be unable to use our services normally;",line17:"2. For additional functionalities to provide our services, you may choose to authorize us to collect and use information. If you refuse to provide it, you will be unable to use the relevant additional functionalities normally or achieve the desired functional effects, but this will not affect your normal use of the basic functionalities of our services.",line18:"The information we collect will be used for the following purposes:",line19:"1. To conduct data statistics and understand operational status;",line20:"2. To analyze how users use this website and their usage habits to improve our services;",line21:"3. To prevent illegal or potentially illegal activities and violations of user guidelines;",line22:"4. Other uses disclosed to you when collecting specific information.",line23:"We would like to particularly remind you:",line24:"1. We are committed to providing diverse services to meet your needs. Due to the wide variety of services we provide, and the differences in specific service choices made by different users, the types and scopes of the personal information collected and used for basic/optional functions may vary, so please refer to the specific service functionalities;",line25:"2. To provide you with a better product and service experience, we are continuously working to improve our services and technology, and we may periodically introduce new or optimized features that may require the collection or use of new personal information or change the purposes or methods of personal information usage. In such cases, we will provide you with detailed explanations through updates to this policy, pop-ups, or page prompts, where you will be given the option to consent, and we will only begin collecting and using after obtaining your explicit consent.",line26:"During this process, if you have any questions, opinions, or suggestions, you can contact us through the contact information provided at the end of this policy, and we will respond to you as soon as possible.",line27:"The specific functional scenarios we provide for you include:",line28:"(1) Assisting you in becoming our user and account management",line29:"1. Basic Services",line30:"When you browse our platform's website (whether registered or logged in), we may automatically collect the following information related to you: your browser type and language, operating system type, broadband type; your IP (Internet Protocol) address, which can sometimes show your geographical location; the pages, ads, and links you have browsed within the website; user behavior logs; the access requests issued when you visit the website and their dates, times, and referral URLs (if any).",line31:"To ensure that we are providing services for you personally, we may verify your identity based on the information you provide above.",line32:"2. Account Management",line33:"Authorized Login: We may share your account information (avatar, nickname, and other page prompt information) with third parties with your consent, allowing you to conveniently register or directly log in through third-party accounts. Additionally, we may obtain your third-party account information from Alibaba Cloud third parties based on your authorization and bind it with your Chuan Shen Community account, enabling you to log in directly and use our services at third parties. We will use your relevant information within the scope of your authorized consent.",line34:"We provide services based on the Chuan Shen Community account. When you create a Chuan Shen Community account, sign our user agreement (CLA), subscribe to our email list, or use related functionalities of the model library and dataset, you need to provide us with at least your mobile number, email address, intended account name and password, avatar, nickname, and gender, browser settings information. If you refuse to provide the above information, we may not be able to provide you with account registration, model upload, online tool usage, comments, and other services, but this will not affect your ability to browse.",line35:"(2) Providing you with favorites and follow features",line36:"During your browsing of our website or client, you can choose to collect models and datasets of interest and establish follow relationships with organizations, research institutions, or other users that you are interested in. In the process of using the above features, we will collect service log information including your favorites and follow relationships for the purpose of achieving the above functions and other purposes that we explicitly inform you.",line37:"You can manage your favorites and follow information in your favorites section or follow list, or delete them.",line38:"(3) Customer service and dispute resolution",line39:"When you contact us or submit a request for after-sales service or dispute resolution, in order to safeguard your account and system security, we need you to provide necessary personal information to verify your user identity.",line40:"To facilitate communication with you, quickly help you solve problems, or record relevant problem-handling solutions and results, we may save your communication records with us, communication/call logs, and related content (including account information, other information you provide to prove relevant facts, or your contact information). If you consult, complain, or provide suggestions regarding specific issues, we will use your account information.",line41:"For the reasonable need to provide services and improve service quality, we may also use other information about you, including information you provide when contacting customer service or questionnaire responses you send us during surveys.",line42:"(4) Providing you with security protection",line43:"To enhance the security of the services provided by us and our affiliated companies and partners, protect you or other users or the public from personal and property security infringements, better prevent security risks such as phishing websites, fraud, network vulnerabilities, computer viruses, cyber attacks, and network intrusions, and more accurately identify violations of laws and regulations or violations of Chuan Shen Community related agreements or rules, we embed an application security SDK developed by our affiliated company in our platform website to collect your device information and service log information, and may use or integrate your user information, device information, and service log information along with information shared by our affiliated companies or partners with your authorization or in accordance with the law to comprehensively assess account risk, perform identity verification, detect and prevent security events, and take necessary recording, analysis, and handling measures in accordance with the law.",line44:"(5) Other rules regarding the collection and use of personal information",line45:"1. If the information you provide contains personal information about other users, you must ensure that you have obtained legal authorization before providing this personal information to the platform. If we use the information for purposes not specified in this privacy policy, or if we use information collected for a specific purpose for other purposes, we will obtain your consent in advance.",line46:"2. If we indirectly obtain your information from a third party, we will clearly request in writing that the third party specify the source of the personal information before collection, and whether they have obtained your legal authorization for their collection, processing, and provision of your personal information to us. Except for circumstances clearly permitted by law, we will only collect your personal information from third parties after confirming that they have obtained your authorization. If the scope of the third party’s authorization cannot cover our processing and use purposes, we will either refrain from processing your personal information or require that third party to obtain your consent before processing your personal information. At the same time, our professional security team will strengthen the security of personal information (including sensitive information reporting, sensitive information encrypted storage, access control, etc.). We will take protective measures and means for indirectly obtained personal information that are no less than those we use to protect our own users' personal information.",line47:"3. Exceptions to obtaining authorization and consent. According to relevant laws and regulations, we may collect and use your personal information without your authorization and consent under the following circumstances:",line48:"(1) Related to our obligations under laws and regulations;",line49:"(2) Directly related to national security and defense;",line50:"(3) Directly related to public safety, public health, and significant public interests;",line51:"(4) Directly related to criminal investigation, prosecution, trial, and judgment execution, etc., in judicial or administrative law enforcement;",line52:"(5) Necessary to protect your or other individuals' life, property, and other major legitimate rights and interests, but it is difficult to obtain consent;",line53:"(6) Personal information publicly disclosed by you to the public;",line54:"(7) Collecting personal information from legally public information, such as legal news reports, government information disclosures, etc.;",line55:"(8) Necessary for fulfilling and performing relevant agreements or other written documents signed with you;",line56:"(9) Necessary for maintaining the safe and stable operation of services provided, such as discovering and handling service failures;",line57:"(10) Other circumstances as specified by laws and regulations.",line58:"Please be informed that, according to applicable laws, if we take technical measures and other necessary measures for the processing of personal information, such that the data recipients are unable to re-identify specific individuals and cannot restore them, or we may conduct de-identification research, statistical analysis, and predictions on the collected information to improve the content and layout of the platform, and enhance our services (including using anonymized data for machine learning or model algorithm training), such processing of data will not require further notification to you or obtaining your consent.",line59:"4. In the event that the platform ceases operations, we will take reasonable measures to ensure the safety of your user information, including but not limited to promptly stopping the collection of user information; a notice of cessation of operations will be delivered individually or announced to users; and the personal information held will be deleted or anonymized.",line60:"II. How We Share, Transfer, and Publicly Disclose Your Personal Information",line61:"(1) Sharing",line62:"Except for the usage conditions stated above, we will not share your personal information with any companies, organizations, or individuals outside this platform, except in the following situations:",line63:"1. Sharing with explicit consent: We will share your personal information with others after obtaining your explicit consent;",line64:"2. Sharing under legal circumstances: We may share your personal information externally as required by laws and regulations, litigation dispute resolution needs, or as requested by administrative or judicial authorities according to the law;",line65:"3. Sharing with authorized partners: We may entrust trusted partners to provide services, and thus we may share certain user information with partners to offer better customer service and optimize user experience. We will only share your user information for legitimate, proper, necessary, specific, and clear purposes, and only share the user information necessary for providing services. Our partners do not have the right to use the shared user information for any other purposes. For the companies, organizations, and individuals with whom we share user information, we will sign strict confidentiality agreements and information protection agreements, requiring them to process user information in accordance with our instructions, this privacy policy, and any other relevant confidentiality and security measures.",line66:"(2) Transfer",line67:"We will not transfer your user information to any companies, organizations, or individuals, except in the following situations:",line68:"1. Transfer with explicit consent: We will transfer your user information to others after obtaining your explicit consent;",line69:"2. In the event of a merger, acquisition, or bankruptcy liquidation with other legal entities, or in other situations involving mergers, acquisitions, or bankruptcy liquidation, if it involves the transfer of user information, we will require the new company or organization holding your user information to continue to be bound by this policy; otherwise, we will ask that company, organization, and individual to seek your authorization and consent again.",line70:"(3) Public Disclosure",line71:"We will only publicly disclose your user information in the following situations:",line72:"1. With your explicit consent or based on your active choice, we may publicly disclose your user information;",line73:"2. To protect the personal and property safety of our and our affiliates' users or the public from infringement, we may disclose your user information according to applicable laws or relevant agreements and rules of this platform.",line74:"(4) Exceptions to obtaining authorization and consent before sharing, transferring, or publicly disclosing user information",line75:"According to relevant laws and regulations, in the following situations, sharing, transferring, or publicly disclosing your user information does not require prior authorization and consent from you:",line76:"1. Related to national security and defense;",line77:"2. Related to public safety, public health, and significant public interests;",line78:"3. Related to criminal investigations, prosecutions, trials, and execution of judgments;",line79:"4. Necessary to safeguard significant legitimate rights and interests of you or other individuals, but it is difficult to obtain consent;",line80:"5. Personal information publicly disclosed by you to the public;",line81:"6. Collecting personal information from legally public information, such as legal news reports, government information disclosures, etc.;",line82:"7. Other situations specified by laws and regulations.",line83:"Please be informed that, according to applicable laws, if we take technical measures and other necessary measures for processing personal information in such a way that the data recipients cannot re-identify specific individuals and cannot restore it, then such data sharing, transfer, and public disclosure do not require further notification to you and obtaining your consent.",line84:"III. How We Store and Protect Your Personal Information Security",line85:"(1) Basic Rules for Data Storage",line86:"We will take reasonable and feasible measures to avoid collecting irrelevant user information. We will only retain your user information for the period necessary to achieve the purposes described in this policy, unless permitted by law. After exceeding the aforementioned retention period of user information, we will delete or anonymize your personal information.",line87:"We will store the collected user information within the territory of the People's Republic of China. If it is necessary to transfer personal information abroad due to business needs under applicable legal provisions, we will obtain your consent in advance and inform you of the purpose, recipient, security measures, and security risks related to the outbound transfer of user information.",line88:"(2) Technical Measures for Data Protection",line89:"We strive to take various reasonable physical, electronic, and managerial security measures to protect your user information from unauthorized access, public disclosure, use, modification, damage, or loss. For example, we provide HTTPS protocol for secure browsing on this platform's website; we will use encryption technology to enhance the security of user information; we will use trusted protection mechanisms to prevent user information from malicious attacks; we will deploy access control mechanisms to ensure that only authorized personnel can access user information.",line90:"(3) Organizational Measures for Data Protection Management",line91:"We have established an industry-leading data-centric data security management system, enhancing the security of personal information from multiple dimensions, including organizational construction, system design, personnel management, and product technology. We will set up a dedicated personal information protection department and conduct security and privacy protection training courses to enhance employees' awareness of the importance of protecting personal information.",line92:"(4) Response to Personal Information Security Incidents",line93:"In case of unfortunate user information security incidents (leakage, loss, etc.), we will promptly inform you in accordance with the requirements of laws and regulations: the basic situation and possible impact of the security incident, the measures we have taken or will take to address it, suggestions for you to prevent and mitigate risks independently, and remedies for you. We will inform you of the incidents via email, letter, phone, push notifications, etc. In cases where it is difficult to inform each user information subject individually, we will take reasonable and effective measures to issue a public announcement. Meanwhile, we will also report the handling of user information security incidents in accordance with the requirements of regulatory authorities.",line94:"(5) You Should Also Protect Your Account Security",line95:"We strongly recommend that you do not use non-recommended communication methods to send your information while using this platform, and please use complex passwords to assist us in protecting your account security.",line96:"Please note that the information you voluntarily share or even publicly share while using this platform may involve personal information of you or others, as well as personal sensitive information. Please consider carefully whether to share or even publicly share relevant information while using this platform.",line97:"IV. How You Manage Your Personal Information",line98:"You can access and manage your personal information in the following ways:",line99:"1. You can log into the platform's website to view and manage (modify or delete) the basic information submitted during your use of the platform's services.",line100:"2. In the following situations, you can request us to delete your user information:",line101:"(1) If our handling of user information violates laws and regulations;",line102:"(2) If we collect or use your user information without obtaining your explicit consent;",line103:"(3) If our handling of personal information severely violates the agreements made with you.",line104:"For security reasons, you may be required to provide a written request or prove your identity in another way. We may ask you to verify your identity before processing your request. For your reasonable requests, we generally do not charge a fee, but for repeated requests that exceed reasonable limits, we may charge a fee at our discretion. We may refuse requests that pertain to information not directly related to your identity, requests that are excessively repeated, or requests that require excessive technical measures (e.g., developing a new system or fundamentally changing existing practices), or requests that could pose risks to the legitimate rights and interests of others or are impractical.",line105:"In the following situations, as required by laws and regulations, we will not be able to respond to your request:",line106:"(1) Related to our performance of legally mandated obligations;",line107:"(2) Directly related to national security and defense;",line108:"(3) Directly related to public safety, public health, and significant public interests;",line109:"(4) Directly related to criminal investigation, prosecution, trial, and execution of judgments;",line110:"(5) Where there is sufficient evidence to indicate that the individual subject of personal information has malicious intent or abuses rights;",line111:"(6) To maintain the significant legitimate rights and interests of the personal information subject or others, but it is very difficult to obtain the person's authorization and consent;",line112:"(7) Responding to your request would seriously harm the legitimate rights and interests of you or others;",line113:"(8) Involving trade secrets.",line114:"&nbsp;&nbsp;1. Each function requires certain basic user information to be completed (see Article 1 of this policy). Besides this, you can contact us through the clarification path in Article 8 of this policy to grant or revoke your consent. When you revoke your consent, we will no longer process the corresponding user information. However, the decision to revoke consent will not affect the processing of user information that was carried out based on your prior authorization.",line115:"&nbsp;&nbsp;2. You can contact us through the clarification path in Article 8 of this policy to submit an account cancellation request (please specify 'Account Cancellation for Chuanxin Community'). After you actively delete your user information, we will stop providing services to you, but we still need to retain the information you provided or generated during your use of the services for the time required by regulatory authorities.",line116:"V. How We Use Cookies and Similar Technologies",line117:"(1) Use of Cookies",line118:"To ensure the normal operation of the website, provide you with a smoother access experience, and recommend content you may be interested in, we store Cookies, Flash Cookies, or other local storage generally containing identifiers, site names, and some numbers and characters on your computer or mobile device (collectively referred to as 'Cookies'). With the help of Cookies, the website is able to store your favorite datasets, models, and other data.",line119:"If your browser or browser extensions allow, you can modify the acceptance level of Cookies or reject our Cookies. However, if you do this, it may affect your secure access to our website in certain cases, and you may need to change user settings each time you visit our website.",line120:"(2) Use of Similar Technologies to Cookies",line121:"In addition to Cookies, we also use web beacons, pixel tags, ETags, and other similar technologies on the website. For example, emails we send to you may contain links to content on our website, and if you click that link, we will track the click to help us understand your service preferences, enabling us to proactively improve the user service experience. Web beacons are usually transparent images embedded in the website or email. With pixel tags in emails, we can determine whether the email was opened. If you do not want your activity to be tracked in this way, you can unsubscribe from our mailing list at any time.",line122:"ETags (Entity Tags) are HTTP protocol headers transferred between internet browsers and internet servers that can replace Cookies. ETags help us avoid unnecessary server load, enhance service efficiency, save resources and energy, while we may use ETags to record your identity so that we can better understand and improve our products or services. Most browsers provide a feature for users to clear browser cache data, which you can use to perform corresponding data clearing operations in your browser's settings. However, please note that if you disable ETags, you may not enjoy an optimal product or service experience.",line123:"VI. Special Provisions for Minor Users' Information",line124:"1. Our services are primarily aimed at adults. If you are a minor, we require you to have your parents or guardians carefully read this privacy policy and use our services or provide us with information only with their consent.",line125:"2. For the collection of personal information of minors with the consent of their parents or guardians, we will only use, share, transfer, or disclose this information in accordance with legal regulations, with the explicit consent of parents or guardians, or when necessary to protect minors.",line126:"3. Important reminder: if you are a child under the age of 14, you and your guardian should carefully read this policy before you use our services, ensuring that you do so only with the consent of your guardian and under their guidance to ensure your safety when using our services and conducting transactions.",line127:"4. If you are the parent or another guardian of a minor, please pay attention to whether the minor under your care is using our services after obtaining your authorized consent. If you have any questions regarding the personal information of the minor you are responsible for, please contact us through the contact details provided in Article 8 of this policy.",line128:"VII. How This Privacy Policy is Updated",line129:"As our services and technology continue to update and optimize in response to the needs of you and other users, we may update our privacy policy to inform you of specific changes when new services or business processes change. However, we will not restrict your rights as provided in this policy without your explicit consent. We will publish any changes made to this policy on our website.",line130:"For significant changes, we will also provide more prominent notifications (including notifications published on this platform's website). Significant changes referred to in this policy include but are not limited to:",line131:"1. Significant changes in our service model, such as the purposes of processing user information, types of user information processed, and ways in which user information is used;",line132:"2. Significant changes in our control, organizational structure, etc., such as changes in ownership due to business adjustments, bankruptcy, acquisitions, etc.;",line133:"3. Changes in the main recipients of shared, transferred, or publicly disclosed user information;",line134:"4. Significant changes in your rights concerning user information processing and the ways in which those rights can be exercised;",line135:"5. Changes in the responsible department for user information security, contact information, and complaint channels;",line136:"6. When user information security impact assessment reports indicate a high risk.",line137:"We may suspend or terminate any features on the platform website, or we may introduce new features or impose restrictions on certain features, or restrict access to some or all services. When we make significant changes to the platform that may adversely affect you, we will make reasonable efforts to notify you. Your continued use of the platform indicates your agreement to the updated privacy policy. If you do not agree to the updated privacy policy, please stop using the platform immediately.",line138:"VIII. How to Contact Us",line139:"If you have any questions, comments, or suggestions regarding the content of this policy, you can contact us using the following methods:",line140:"Email: {email}",line141:"If you are not satisfied with our response, especially if you believe that our handling of personal information has harmed your legitimate rights, you may also seek resolution by filing a lawsuit in a court with jurisdiction where the defendant resides."},recharge:{line1:"Dear User, this agreement is entered into by and between you (hereinafter referred to as 'User') and this Recharge Service Platform (hereinafter referred to as 'Platform'), to clearly define the rights and obligations of both parties. Please read and understand all the contents of this agreement before using the recharge services provided by this platform. By clicking the 'Agree' button, you voluntarily agree to accept all the terms and conditions of this agreement, as well as any policies, guidelines, notices, norms, or terms published by the platform. If you do not accept any part or all of this agreement, please do not click the 'Agree' button.",line2:"I. Acceptance and Effectiveness of the Agreement",line3:"1.1 Before using the recharge services provided by this platform, the user must carefully read and agree to all the contents of this agreement.By clicking the 'Agree' button, the user signifies that they have fully accepted and agreed to all the terms of this agreement.",line4:"1.2 This agreement takes effect from the date of its signing, establishing a contractual relationship between the user and the platform.",line5:"1.3 The user understands and accepts that the platform may revise the content of this agreement from time to time in response to changes in national laws and regulations or the needs of actual business operations, and such revisions will be announced in advance on the software system, website, or other platforms of the platform. The revised terms will take effect on the date specified in the announcement. If the user continues to use the recharge services, it indicates that they agree to and accept the revised agreement. If the user does not agree to the revised terms, they should immediately stop using the recharge services or cease using the service.",line6:"II. Overview of Recharge Services",line7:"2.1 The recharge services provided by the platform refer to the user recharging a certain amount of money into their account through the recharge channels provided by the platform, to be used for shopping, payment, or other relevant activities on the platform.",line8:"2.2 The platform will automatically configure an account for the user, and the user agrees to complete real-name verification, after which they can officially use the recharge services.",line9:"III. Methods and Rules for User Recharge",line10:"3.1 When the user recharges their account, this act is deemed as the user prepaying the service fee to the platform. This prepaid service fee will be used to purchase various products or services provided by the platform.",line11:"3.2 The user can recharge their account through the recharge channels provided by the platform, including but not limited to bank cards, Alipay, WeChat, etc. Once the recharge is successful, it takes effect immediately and cannot be refunded.",line12:"3.3 The user must use their own legal funds and charge within the amount range specified by the platform. Amounts exceeding the specified range will not be accepted.",line13:"3.4 Any amounts, coupons, or discounts obtained by the user through platform promotions will be used according to the platform's usage rules, and the user cannot request invoices for these amounts.",line14:"3.5 The user must ensure that the recharge information provided is true, accurate, and complete. Any loss or failure due to incorrect information will be borne by the user.",line15:"3.6 The user agrees to ensure that the account opened in their name is not used by a third party. Any operation or consumption based on this account will be deemed as the user's own actions, and the platform will not be held responsible for this.",line16:"3.7 The platform has the right to impose restrictions on the user's account in the recharge service according to business needs or regulatory requirements, including but not limited to limiting the amount, prohibiting consumption, legally withholding funds, or even suspending the account.",line17:"3.8 The user understands and agrees that the platform will not issue invoices based on the recharge amount. The platform will only issue invoices based on the actual consumption amount of the user on the platform (if the consumption includes amounts obtained through platform promotions, the platform has the right to not issue invoices for these amounts). If the user requests a refund for returned or exchanged products or services, they must first return the issued invoice to the platform and cooperate with the platform to reverse the invoice before they can receive a refund within the platform's specified time.",line18:"3.9 If the user entrusts a third party to recharge their account, the user must ensure that they understand and trust the third party, and that the third party is aware and agrees to accept the user's entrustment for the recharge. Otherwise, if the platform is informed by the third party that the recharge was not authorized, the platform has the right to immediately lock the user's account (during the lock period, the platform will suspend the user's service). Within 30 days from the date the user's account is locked, the user must provide sufficient evidence to confirm that the third party agreed to the recharge. Otherwise, the user agrees and authorizes the platform to cooperate with the third party's request, to return the corresponding funds from the locked user account. If the user's account balance is insufficient to return the funds, the user agrees to top up the necessary amount within 30 days, authorize the platform to return the funds, or return the funds from their WeChat account or Alipay account. Unless the third party agrees that the user does not need to return this amount.",line19:"IV. User Rights and Obligations",line20:"4.1 The user has the right to recharge in accordance with the platform's specified methods, and the platform will promptly credit the corresponding virtual currency to the user's account based on the actual recharge amount.",line21:"4.2 The user must ensure that the payment account used for recharging is legal and valid and bears all responsibilities arising from violating this obligation.",line22:"4.3 The user must comply with the platform's regulations and national laws and regulations when using recharge services, and must not use recharge services for any illegal activities. If the user's use of recharge services results in any loss to the platform, the user shall bear full liability for compensation.",line23:"4.4 Virtual currency obtained by the user after a successful recharge cannot be withdrawn and can only be used for consumption within the platform.",line24:"4.5 The user should properly manage their account and recharge-related information to prevent unauthorized use of the account by others.",line25:"4.6 If the user suffers any damage to their rights due to their own operational errors, lack of understanding of the recharge billing method, or other reasons, the responsibility lies with the user.",line26:"4.7 If the user encounters any issues or questions while using recharge services, they should promptly contact the platform's customer service for assistance and solutions.",line27:"V. Exemption and Dispute Resolution",line28:"5.1 The platform shall not be liable for any losses, delays, or other adverse effects suffered by the user during the use of recharge services, including but not limited to losses caused by network failures, technical issues, or operational errors.",line29:"5.2 If the user encounters any disputes or controversies while using recharge services, they should first resolve them through friendly negotiations with the platform. If negotiations fail, either party has the right to file a lawsuit in the competent court with jurisdiction over the platform's location.",line30:"VI. Termination of the Agreement",line31:"6.1 The user can terminate this agreement at any time, and after termination, they will no longer be entitled to the recharge service benefits under this agreement.",line32:"6.2 The platform has the right to terminate this agreement if the user violates the terms of this agreement or the national laws and regulations, and reserves the right to take legal action to hold the user accountable.",line33:"VII. Modification of the Agreement",line34:"7.1 The platform has the right to modify this agreement at any time, and the modified agreement will take effect once it is published. The user's continued use of the recharge services indicates that they have fully accepted and agreed to the modified agreement.",line35:"7.2 If the user does not agree to the modified terms, they can stop using the recharge services.",line36:"VIII. Other Clauses",line37:"8.1 The establishment, performance, interpretation, and dispute resolution of this agreement are governed by the laws of the People's Republic of China.",line38:"8.2 If any provision of this agreement is deemed invalid or unenforceable, it will not affect the validity of the other provisions.",line39:"This agreement takes effect from the date the user clicks 'Agree'."}},autohub:{banner:{title:"AutoHub - Your Browser Helper",subtitle:"Say goodbye to boring web tasks! and Research Support",part1P1Title:"Empower On-Campus Training:",part1P1Desc:"Provides real large model training and deployment environments for courses like AI, machine learning, and deep learning, allowing students to gain hands-on experience and deepen understanding.",part1P2Title:"Support for Graduation Projects and Innovation Programs:",part1P2Desc:"Offers unified model library, dataset management, and an efficient AI application development platform to help students manage models, data, and code in their projects and accelerate innovation.",part1P3Title:"Support for Competitions:",part1P3Desc:"Provides a stable and high-performance platform for AI competitions, allowing students to focus on algorithm and model innovation.",part1P4Title:"Accelerate Advanced Research:",part1P4Desc:"Faculty and researchers can fine-tune large models using CSGHub with advantages like multi-source model sync and secure private deployment, accelerating research and boosting efficiency.",part2Title:"Comprehensive Asset Management and Agent Ecosystem",part2P1Title:"Unified Management of Models/Data/Code:",part2P1Desc:"A one-stop hub for managing all core AI assets with support for large files, online editing, and dataset preview, keeping everything organized.",part2P2Title:"Powerful AgenticOps Support:",part2P2Desc:"Built-in prompt engineering and MCP capability management with Agent hosting support, helping students and teachers quickly build and deploy intelligent agents.",part2P3Title:"Security and Compliance:",part2P3Desc:"Fully private deployment options ensure all teaching and research data runs securely on-premise, meeting universities’ strict data privacy and security standards.",part3Title:"Data Security Assurance",part3P1Title:"Fully Private Deployment:",part3P1Desc:"We offer fully private deployment to ensure all teaching and research data is run securely on local servers without relying on external networks.",part3P2Title:"Data Integrity Verification:",part3P2Desc:"CSGHub includes data integrity checks to prevent tampering or loss.",part3P3Title:"High Availability and Disaster Recovery:",part3P3Desc:"We offer HA and DR solutions to ensure stable platform operation and safeguard your data assets.",part3P4Title:"MCP Security Scan:",part3P4Desc:"Security scans are automatically triggered when MCP is updated or called, detecting potential tool poisoning attacks to ensure secure agent execution.",part4Title:"Efficient and Flexible Compute Management",part4P1Title:"On-Demand Compute Infrastructure:",part4P1Desc:"CSGHub offers powerful resource scheduling. It allocates matched compute instances and preloads the environment, data, and files. Once the project ends, resources are released and saved automatically.",part4P2Title:"Granular Resource Allocation:",part4P2Desc:"Supports fine-grained control over usage duration and access scope for optimized resource usage.",part5Title:"Domestic Model and Ecosystem Compatibility",part5P1Title:"Support for Major Domestic Models:",part5P1Desc:"Compatible with major domestic models like DeepSeek, Qwen, Baichuan, Zhipu, InternLM, and more, easily integrated into existing university ecosystems.",part5P2Title:"Wide Heterogeneous Compute Compatibility:",part5P2Desc:"Supports NVIDIA GPUs, Huawei Ascend, Cambricon, Iluvatar, Enflame, Kunlun, Hygon chips, and both x86 and ARM, meeting diverse campus deployment needs."},plan:{title:"CSGHub Campus Cooperation and Academic Support Plan",desc:"Special support policies for universities and research institutes to advance open-source model and AI tool applications.",part1Title:"Free Enterprise Edition Access",part1Desc:"Universities can apply for the free Enterprise Edition (for non-commercial use), including model management, data processing, and Agent development. Supports private or cloud deployment.",part2Title:"Academic and Teaching Support",part2Desc:"We offer model experiment platforms, daily papers, prompt engineering, and more to support your research. This ecosystem, based on AgenticOps methodology, aims to provide enterprises with low-threshold, efficient, flexible, and innovative AI solutions, helping them achieve continuous AI transformation quickly.",sectionTitle:"Full Lifecycle Management of Agents",sectionContent:"Enterprises can easily start and manage agents using natural language, simplifying the AI transformation process and Our mission is to empower everyone with large models, gather AI industry forces, and provide end-to-end solutions for enterprises with Agents. This methodology is called AgenticOps.",text2:"Our community has over three million users, and the number of supported models ranks among the top globally. Our product suite includes: CSGHub Large Model Asset Management Platform (open-source privatized Hugging Face), which is the Ops part of AgenticOps; and CSGShip Agent Platform (Agent Builder), which is the Agentic part of AgenticOps. Our clients cover more than 30 countries and over 50 industries, including telecom, banking, securities, gaming, new energy, embodied intelligence, and Web3.",text3:"Through our innovative methodology and solutions, OpenCSG helps enterprises fully leverage large model technologies, address core challenges in AI transformation, and promote sustainable and healthy growth. 体验","subpart1-":"大模型一体机提供了持续迭代和随意更换的大模型服务，让您尽情释放创造力，定制属于自己的独特AI体验。无论是聊天、中文写作还是其他应用场景，我们的产品将为您打开无限可能的大门。",subpart2:"数据安全保障，掌握敏感信息的安心选择","subpart2-":"在崇尚数据安全的时代，我们深知您对于隐私保护的重视。大模型一体机支持本地化部署，确保您的大模型和数据始终在安全可控的环境下运行。选择我们的产品，让您的敏感信息得到最可靠的保护。",subpart3:"轻松部署，高效迁移至云服务或其他硬件","subpart3-":"借助容器化部署技术，大模型一体机可以轻松迁移到其他硬件设备或云服务平台。无论是需要弹性扩展能力还是使用云计算资源，我们为您提供简单快捷的解决方案，确保您的大模型始终处于最佳状态。",subpart4:"开源框架，助力开发者构建自定义应用程序","subpart4-":"大模型一体机基于开源框架，为开发者们提供了优秀的工具和平台，助力他们构建和定制化大模型的应用程序。无论您是寻找创新突破点还是希望提供个性化服务，我们都会与您携手共进，实现共同的价值目标。",subpart5:"满足国产化需求，引领行业数字化转型","subpart5-":"大模型一体机兼容国产化硬件，适配信创体系，为金融、政府、运营商等传统企业提供国产化的Al解决方案。通过与信创生态的全面适配，我们致力于推动行业的数字化转型，让国产CPU的算力与大模型的兼容性完美结合。"},xnet:{title:"企业级智能原生基础架构全栈联合方案",sectionTitle:"统一的数据与模型底座",sectionContent:"Dell PowerScale 的智能存储与 OpenCSG 的自研数据互联协议，在训练、分析、生产全链路共享数据与模型，减少复制迁移，全面提升流转效率。",sectionTitle2:"GPU 算力的最大化释放",sectionContent2:"Dell PowerScale 直连 GPU 消除 I/O 瓶颈，结合 OpenCSG 的智能调度，实测可让算力利用率提升 40%+。",sectionTitle3:"可追踪的智能工程化流水线",sectionContent3:"以 CSGHub 为核心的平台能力，将 AgenticOps八阶段串成可追踪、可复现、可自动化的智能工程化闭环，让 AI 项目从 PoC 到生产可控、透明、可规模化。",sectionTitle4:"统一的智能原生架构",sectionContent4:"从个人开发到企业级集群环境保持一致，让企业告别“PoC 跑得飞快，上生产全崩溃”的常见痛点，让 PoC 到生产再无断层。"},AIImg:{headline:"OpenCSG Imagen AI 智能图像助手",desc:"OpenCSG Imagen AI 智能图像助手是一款在 Stable Diffusion 2.1开源模型基础上研发的，致力于增强人类智能的视觉工具集，旨在为创作者提供更好用的工具，催化灵感的萌芽，并实现创意和生产力的指数级放大。OpenCSG Imagen AI 智能图像助手适应专业设计工作流程，提供多种功能。",demo:"快速体验",part1:"文生图绘图模式","part1-":"无需专业绘画技能，用户只需简单描述即可在几秒内生成高质量的画作。",part2:"图生图绘图模式","part2-":"选择多种绘画风格和艺术家参考，通过借鉴不同风格的作品进行创作，提升设计质量和出图效率。",part3:"基于文字描述生成图片","part3-":"根据用户提供的文字描述生成对应的图片，拓展创作可能性。",part4:"基于文字引导修改图片","part4-":"用户可以使用文字引导来修改已有的图片，使其符合自己的创作需求。",part5:"艺术家作品风格迁移","part5-":"通过学习不同艺术家或风格的作品并将其融合，生成具有特定艺术风格的图像，帮助创作者探索全新表达方式。",part6:"图片超分辨率","part6-":"OpenCSG Image AI 图像助手提高画面分辨率，获得更清晰、细节更丰富的图像"},programming:{headline:"OpenCSG StarCode AI 智能编程助手",desc:"StarCode AI 是一款全能的智能编程助手，通过基于大模型技术的创新功能，帮助开发者提高工作效率。它融合了其它辅助编程助手的优点，并为开发者提供更多强大的功能。",demo:"快速体验",part1:"代码自动生成和补全","part1-":"支持根据自然语言注释自动生成代码，以及根据已有的代码生成后续代码，使得编码过程更加快速和准确。OpenCSG StarCode AI 还进一步提供了针对当前行或生成后续若干行代码的补全功能，帮助开发者轻松完成编程任务。",part2:"自动生成单元测试","part2-":"支持根据常见单元测试框架生成单元测试以及单元测试相关代码解释。 StarCode AI 还进一步提供了针对当前行或生成后续若干行代码的补全功能，帮助开发者轻松完成编程任务。",part3:"研发领域知识问答","part3-":"支持智能问答功能，开发者可以直接向助手提出技术问题，无需离开集成开发环境 (IDE) 去搜索引擎寻找答案。这种无缝集成的体验让开发者能够更专注地解决问题，提高工作效率。 StarCode AI 还进一步提供了针对当前行或生成后续若干行代码的补全功能，帮助开发者轻松完成编程任务。",part4:"代码翻译","part4-":"不仅支持多种主流编程语言之间的互译，还借鉴了 CoPilot 的功能，基于人工智能大模型实现了更准确、高质量的代码翻译。 StarCode AI 还进一步提供了针对当前行或生成后续若干行代码的补全功能，帮助开发者轻松完成编程任务。"}},dailyPaper:{paper:"论文中心",eachpaper:"精选论文推荐",arxiv:"最新的新闻、技术和资源",newpaper:"发布新的社区论文",placeholder:"按标题搜索",goodpaper:"贡献优质社区论文",recommendUser:"将您发现的优质论文推荐给 CSGHub",cardDesc:"CSGHub是一个开源、可信的大模型资产管理平台，可帮助用户治理LLM和LLM应用生命周期中涉及到的资产（数据集、模型文件、代码等）。CSGHub提供类似私有化的Huggingface功能，以类似OpenStack Glance管理虚拟机镜像、Harbor管理容器镜像以及Sonatype Nexus管理制品的方式，实现对LLM资产的管理。",cardTitle2:"开源 Inference",cardDesc2:"开源Inference是一个发布和管理llm推理的平台，为模型部署提供了广泛的开箱即用功能，如UI、RESTful API、自动缩放、计算资源管理、监控等。",cardTitle3:"开源 finetune",cardDesc3:"大模型训练框架，支持lora、全参数微调等，通过定义yaml文件指定基础模型、数据集和训练参数，轻松开启您的大模型训练和微调工作。",cardTitle4:"开源 StarCloud",cardDesc4:"StarCloud是LLM Jobs的开源web界面。调度引擎使用Slurm作为默认引擎，它基于GPU资源执行大型模型作业调度。",cardTitle5:"开源 CSGHubSDK",cardDesc5:"CSGHub SDK 是一个强大的 Python 客户端，专门设计用于与 CSGHub 服务器无缝交互。这个工具包旨在为 Python 开发者提供一个高效且直接的方法来操作和管理远程 CSGHub 实例。无论您是希望自动化任务、管理数据，还是将 CSGHub 功能集成到您的 Python 应用中，CSGHUB SDK 都提供了一整套功能，让您轻松实现目标。",cardTitle6:"开源 CSGHubServer",cardDesc6:"CSGHub Server是开源大模型资产管理平台CSGHub的服务端部分的开源项目，提供基于REST API的模型和数据集等大模型资产管理功能。",cardTitle7:"开源 CSG-VL",cardDesc7:"CSG-VL是一个小型但强大的多模态模型家族。它提供多种即插即用的视觉编码器，如EVA-CLIP、SigLIP，包括Wukong-1B、Llama-3-8B、Phi-1.5、StableLM-2、Qwen1.5和Phi-2",cardTitle8:"开源 开源生态",apply:"申请",login:"登录"},about:{titlePrefix:"关于",titleBrand:"OpenCSG",paragraphs:["OpenCSG（开放传神）是全球领先的开源大模型生态平台，致力于打造开放、协同、可持续的 AI 开发者生态。我们以 AgenticOps AI 原生方法论为核心，构建了覆盖模型研发、资产管理、智能体构建、算力调度的全栈 AI 基础设施体系。","核心产品 CSGHub（AI 资产管理与运营平台）与 AgenticHub（AI 原生智能体开发与运行平台）已帮助众多企业、园区和城市客户构建可管理、可部署、可运营的 AI 能力底座。","截至目前，OpenCSG 开源社区已汇聚超过 20 万个高质量 AI 模型，吸引了来自全球数十个国家和地区的开发者、科研机构与企业用户，技术生态深度渗透金融、政务、制造、医疗、媒体等多个关键行业。"]},benefits:{title:"成为 OpenCSG 合作伙伴，你将获得什么",description:"OpenCSG 渠道生态体系基于企业实际需求，提供四种合作伙伴类型，每种类型具有明确的定位与适用场景，欢迎具有行业资源优势、客户服务能力和技术交付能力的优质伙伴加入。",items:[{title:"全栈 AI 技术赋能",description:"依托 OpenCSG 全栈 AI 基础设施，合作伙伴可获得从模型管理、数据处理到智能体开发部署的端到端技术能力。CSGHub 提供 20 万+ 高质量模型资源与业界领先的资产管理能力，AgenticHub 则以无代码与代码双模式降低智能体开发门槛，帮助伙伴快速构建面向行业场景的 AI 解决方案，无需从零搭建底层技术栈。"},{title:"共享 20 万+ 模型生态与全球开发者社区",description:"合作伙伴可接入 OpenCSG 覆盖自然语言处理、计算机视觉、多模态等核心方向的丰富模型库，以及连接全球数十个国家和地区开发者、科研机构与企业的活跃社区资源。通过开放架构持续连接模型生态、开发者生态与算力生态，合作伙伴能够借助社区力量加速产品创新与技术迭代。"},{title:"联合解决方案与商业化落地支持",description:"OpenCSG 为合作伙伴提供从方案共创到市场推广的全链路支持。我们将与伙伴共同打造面向金融、政务、制造、医疗等行业的联合解决方案，提供技术对接、方案验证与产品适配支持，并开放联合品牌营销、行业展会、客户线索共享等市场资源，帮助伙伴实现从技术能力到商业变现的高效闭环。"},{title:"品牌背书与行业影响力",description:"作为在大模型开源生态领域建立了广泛行业影响力的平台，OpenCSG 已服务众多头部企业客户与生态伙伴。合作伙伴可借助 OpenCSG 的品牌势能与行业口碑，提升自身在 AI 领域的市场认知度与客户信任度，在竞争中获得差异化优势。"},{title:"专属技术培训与认证体系",description:"OpenCSG 为合作伙伴提供系统化的技术培训与官方认证，涵盖平台使用、方案开发、部署运维等核心能力，帮助伙伴团队快速掌握 AI 基础设施的应用方法，建立专业技术服务能力，获得面向客户的官方资质背书。"}]},types:{title:"合作伙伴类型",description:"无论你是技术型团队、渠道型伙伴还是行业方案商，OpenCSG 都为你提供匹配的合作模式，找到最适合你的生态位置。",items:[{title:"类型一：生态合作伙伴",description:"与技术能力互补的企业建立深度生态合作，围绕模型、算力、数据、应用等方向进行联合创新，共同丰富 OpenCSG 开源 AI 基础设施的技术生态。适合芯片厂商、云服务商、数据服务商、AI 技术公司等。"},{title:"类型二：经销商",description:"作为 OpenCSG 产品与方案的渠道推广伙伴，负责向区域或行业客户进行产品销售与市场拓展，享受专属商务政策与渠道支持。适合具备区域客户资源或行业渠道能力的销售型公司。"},{title:"类型三：OEM 厂商",description:"将 OpenCSG 的 AI know-how 和软件开发能力的独立软件开发商。"}]},logos:{title:"合作伙伴"},process:{title:"如何成为 OpenCSG 合作伙伴",description:"我们为合作伙伴设计了清晰高效的入驻流程，从提交申请到合作落地，全程提供专属团队支持。",items:[{title:"第一步：注册过程",description:"提交合作申请。将公司名称、所在地区、联络方式、合作意向及业务简介发送至 OpenCSG 商务合作邮箱，或通过官网合作伙伴申请入口在线提交申请信息。OpenCSG 合作团队将在收到申请后对合作伙伴的资质进行评估，包括行业经验、技术能力、市场资源及合作意向匹配度等维度，并在约定时间内反馈审核结果，与您确认适合的合作伙伴类型。"},{title:"第二步：学习过程",description:"确认合作伙伴类型后，OpenCSG 官方将为合作伙伴提供全方位的培训服务，包括 Academy 学院课程、销售手册、讲师培训等多种形式，帮助合作伙伴团队系统掌握 OpenCSG 产品体系、行业解决方案与商务政策，建立面向客户的专业服务能力。"},{title:"第三步：合作伙伴认证",description:"参与完课程培训后，即可获得 OpenCSG 官方合作伙伴认证证书。双方就合作内容、权益义务、商务政策等达成一致后，正式签署合作协议，确立合作伙伴关系。"},{title:"第四步：合作启动与赋能落地",description:"OpenCSG 等）的数据抓取工具",fineweb_edu_chinese_common_internal_dec:"用户可以定义自己的评分标准，根据这些标准对数据源中的数据进行评分，并过滤数据。最高分是5分。",smoltalk_chinese_common_internal_dec:"使用固定的system_prompt生成具有大型模型的相关多轮对话并对其进行评分。根据用户指定的分数过滤数据，只保留分数最高的数据。",cosmopedia_chinese_preprocess_internal_dec:"使用MakeCosmopediaMapper操作符将原始文本转换为WikiHow样式的详细教程。此工具调用大型语言模型，根据输入的种子文本生成结构化教程内容。",md_to_jsonl_preprocess_internal_dec:"使用分块支持将MD文件转换为JSONL格式。",modelNotAvailable:"此模型不可用",searchingModel:"正在验证模型...",searchSelectHint:"支持输入模型名称进行远程搜索",selectModel:"选择模型",moreModels:"更多模型",searchModelName:"搜索模型名称",fetchModelListError:"获取模型列表失败",streamingModeBenefits:"流式模式的优势：",streamingBenefit1:"显著降低内存占用，适合大规模数据处理",streamingBenefit2:"边读边处理，提升数据处理效率",streamingBenefit3:"支持处理超大文件，不受内存限制",executionModeTitle:"执行模式选择",executionMode:"执行模式",normalMode:"普通模式",streamingMode:"流式模式",normalExecution:"普通",streamingExecution:"流式",streamingCompatibleDetected:"检测到您的工作流中所有算子均支持流式模式",spaceResourceConfig:"空间资源配置"},prompts:{source:"来源",loading:"加载中...",relationModel:"关联模型：",relationPrompts:"关联提示词库",openUp:"展开",closeUp:"收起",updateAt:"变更时间：",placeholder:"搜索",placeholderLang:"语言",placeholderTag:"场景",promptLibrary:"提示词库",noData:"无数据",optimizationAssistant:"优化助手",modelComparison:"模型对比",promptContent:"内容",copy:"复制",edit:"编辑",title:"标题",history:"历史记录",historyChat:"历史对话",newConversation:"新建对话",edit:"编辑",del:"删除",chatTip:"我可以帮助您生成或者优化提示词！",chatTip1:"请点击历史对话或者新建对话。",reAnswer:"重新生成",send:"发送",startChat:"开始对话",titlePlaceholder:"请输入标题",newPrompt:"新建提示词",newPromptsList:"新建提示词库",settings:"设置",noPrompt:"当前暂无提示词",promptsName:"提示词库名称",promptsNameTips:"应用于提示库名称展示，创建后不可更改",promptsNickName:"提示词库别名",promptsDesc:"提示词库介绍",relatedModel:"关联模型",editPrompt:"编辑提示词",delTitle:"删除内容",delContent:"你确定要删除该提示词吗?此操作不能撤消",getListFail:"获取提示词历史失败",applySuccess:"提示词已复制",tag:{Image:"图像生成",Video:"视频生成",Music:"音乐生成",Code:"代码生成",DataAnalysis:"数据分析",Research:"研究",Personalization:"个性化",CreativeWriting:"创意写作",TechnicalWriting:"技术写作",Design:"设计",Marketing:"市场营销",Education:"教育"},tips:{tip1:"帮我优化图片生成的提示：一只可爱的小狗",ti
